# Worklog

---
Task ID: 1
Agent: Z.ai Code (main orchestrator)
Task: Build the complete "Mirror Entity Laboratory" responsive web app (Next.js 16 + TS + Tailwind 4 + shadcn/ui + Framer Motion + zustand + LLM-backed transmissions)

Work Log:
- Designed the cosmic design system in `src/app/globals.css`: dark deep-space tokens (#05040B base, purple/indigo/cyan/pink atmosphere vars), light ethereal-lab tokens (#F2FAFF base, #1264B0 primary), hairline/glass/glow utilities, mono-label typography, hero + title text gradients (pink→gold in dark, blue→cyan in light), calm keyframes (drift, dot-pulse, pill-breathe, shimmer, rise-in), scrollbar styling, reduced-motion support, temporary `.theme-anim` class for 460ms theme transitions.
- Root layout: Plus Jakarta Sans + JetBrains Mono via next/font, ThemeProvider (next-themes, class attribute, dark default, storageKey `mirror-entity-theme`), metadata/viewport for the brand.
- Data architecture in `src/lib/`: `mirror-types.ts` + `data/civilizations.ts` (15 families = 514 reps, full dossiers), `data/interdimensional.ts` (6 orders = 106), `data/professions.ts` (9 domains = 1072, 54 professions with blurbs+details), `data/federation.ts` (10 bodies, 6 treaties, 6 principles), `data/science.ts` (modes, fusion fields, directions, mode context lines, 6 suggested questions, 8 gift lines).
- State: zustand store `src/lib/mirror-store.ts` (mode, science field/direction, sidebar tab, search, modals, mobile nav, query, view, transmission status/record, activeQuery, error, composerFocusNonce; askMirror POST flow; resetField).
- Components in `src/components/mirror/`: AppShell, TopNavigation, ThemeToggle (CSS icon swap, static aria-label to avoid hydration mismatch), Sidebar (+SidebarContent, rows, RefineRealityCard), MobileSidebar (Sheet), ModeSelector, ScienceFilters (AnimatePresence, wraps on desktop, scrolls on mobile), HeroPanel, QuestionCards, StatusBar, QueryComposer (auto-resize textarea, Enter/Shift+Enter, focus glow, short mobile placeholder), TransmissionView (loading phases + shimmer, query echo, luminous vertical line, signature styling, classification badges + honesty notes, return button), ModalShell (glass dialog, mobile full-screen sheet), FederationModal (3 tabs), AstralJobsModal (3-level drill-down: domains → professions → detail + ask), DossierModal (context note per spec §31, ask CTA).
- Atmosphere: CosmicBackdrop (4 drifting radial nebulae, theme-aware vars) + StarField canvas (sparse low-opacity stars, slow twinkle/drift, dark-only, reduced-motion aware).
- Backend: `src/app/api/transmission/route.ts` — z-ai-web-dev-sdk chat completion with a Mirror Entity system prompt (voice rules, honest epistemic framing, strict JSON {classification, transmission}), fence-tolerant JSON extraction, classification whitelist, graceful 400/500 errors.
- Debugged: (1) Turbopack serving stale globals.css — fixed by killing the old dev server and restarting with cleared `.next` (note: globals.css edits require dev-server restart in this environment); (2) ThemeToggle hydration mismatch — static labels + CSS-driven icons; (3) react-hooks/set-state-in-effect lint errors — removed mounted-effect and modal-open reset effect.
- Verified via agent-browser across desktop (1440×900) and mobile (390×844): rendering, golden-path interactions (mode switching, science filters, search filtering + empty states, dossier/Federation/Astral modals incl. ESC close, question→composer fill+focus, Enter-send → loading → ready transmission with correct classification, theme toggle in both directions, toasts for gift + reset, mobile drawer + full-screen modals), zero console errors, clean `bun run lint`, `POST /api/transmission 200` in dev.log.

Stage Summary:
- Complete, browser-verified premium product; all spec sections 1–34 implemented (branding, layout hierarchy, both themes, glassmorphism, micro-animations, responsive rules, search, mode state, transmission experience, factual/speculative distinction, accessibility).
- Key artifacts: `src/components/mirror/*` (17 components), `src/lib/data/*` (archive content), `src/lib/mirror-store.ts`, `src/app/api/transmission/route.ts`, redesigned `globals.css`.
- Operational note: restart dev server (clearing `.next`) after editing `globals.css` — Turbopack does not hot-invalidate it here.

- ENCYCLOPEDIA KIT (ProfileBits.tsx): replaced the block ProfileGallery (16/9 hero + two 4/3 tiles — the "half-screen image") with ProfileFigure, a floated plate system: variant "lead" (float right, 46% / max 248px, aspect 4/5) sits beside the opening identity text; variant "inline" (float right 52% / max 280px, aspect 4/3, clear:both) alternates sides down the page — the prose wraps around every figure exactly like a printed encyclopedia. `.encyc-figure-*` / `.encyc-body::after` (float clear) added to globals.css (mobile media query widens plates to 52/58%). distinctChains rewritten to a sequential reservation walk: earlier figures keep their full fallback chain (own painting → graceful master fallback), later chains drop every source an earlier figure could show — no image can ever render twice on one page, even when a primary painting is missing. FactTile value 13→14.5px (label 10px), ProfileSection label 11.5px, ContextNote 14px + optional className.
- ALL SIX PROFILE PAGES reblended (literally every profile): EntityDossier (removed the old circular avatar — the lead portrait plate IS the portrait; identity/back flow around it; kin plate beside sections 01–04, milieu plate beside 06–07; At-a-glance/gifts/teaching/contact/quote/context clear-both), GroupDossier (single encyc-body flow: back → lead family portrait → badges → sections, milieu beside 04–06, homelands beside 07+, representatives section cleared), SpeciesModal (lead own-painting → chips/facts, realms plate beside 01–02, hall plate beside 03, back button added), TechnologyModal TechDetail (lead tech-<id> painting beside plaque+whisper, craft plate beside 02–03, workshop plate beside 04–05, at-a-glance/meters/adjacent/consult cleared), FederationModal CardDetail (lead emblem, milieu beside description, chambers before the ask; heroFailed state removed), AstralJobsModal Level-3 (lead job painting, domain plate beside detail/mandate, workplace plate inside dossier after pathway; plates computed with null-safe guards, cn import dropped).
- UNIQUE IMAGES: every page builds its three chains with sceneImageFor/sceneImageForDistinct avoid-lists + distinctChains (verified in browser: entity 3/3 unique, group 3/3, tech 3/3, species graceful fam-inner-earth fallback when the ie- painting is not yet painted and still zero repeats).
- CARDS (larger + more info): RegisterRow (avatar 9→12 rounded-xl, name 14.5→16px semibold, registry line right-aligned 11px, split meta into "group · origin · density" 14px + separate italic specialty line 13.5px), TechCard (name 16px, family 10.5px, "origin · noun" 13.5px + NEW line-clamp-2 italic whisper), Federation CardRow (name 15.5px, description 15px), AstralJobs domain cards (title 15px, desc 13.5px) + profession rows (name 15.5px, blurb 13.5px), Sidebar SpeciesRow (name 15px + NEW truncated hall line 11.5px, letter avatar 6×6).
- BACK BUTTON: SpeciesModal gained "Back to the register" (data-testid species-back → closeModal) and GroupDossierBody gained the same at the top of its page — every profile now has an explicit back (entity "Back to {name}", tech "Back to the register", federation "Back to the federation record", professions chevron-to-domain all pre-existing). No new i18n keys needed (reused existing keys everywhere).
- COMMUNION LIGHT RESPONSES: `.communion-reading` reworked from the dark violet panel to luminous light vellum (#f4eede radial washes, warm border, deep shadow, gold hairline); MirrorTransmission text now ink-on-paper (#2a2136 body, #1d1430 italic opening, #7a5a1e gold signature) — 15.5/16.5px. CRITICAL FIX found in browser: the root `overflow-hidden` view was being scrolled by scrollIntoView (Task 28-d top-anchor logic climbs ancestors), pushing the header 108px out of view — changed to overflow-x/y-clip (unscrollable) so the header and composer always stay put.
- STAR PLAY TAROT STORIES: all 39 SUITS + 37 ASPECTS messages rewritten as luminous two-sentence micro-stories in second person (the composed card message = a 4-sentence small tale that assists the reader; nothing ever names the card — including the old "Unnamed Card" self-reference). Card face: artwork 45%→34%, story text 12.5px→16px serif italic white/95 with violet glow text-shadow, seat label kept tiny; cards are taller on sm+ (h-[520px]) so the full story fits. Verified: three flipped cards, three unique stories, three unique paintings.
- ORACLE PAPER: `.starplay-reading` → `.oracle-paper` (ivory sheet, starlight fibers, warm ink) with a "sent in front of you" entrance (y40/scale .93/rotateX 12 → front, transformPerspective 1000); title + signature in aged-gold ink, body in warm dark serif, "Woven from" chips restyled as ink-on-paper. API SYSTEM_PROMPT gained "HOW TO WRITE — VERY HUMAN, VERY INSPIRING": warm wise human voice, plain beautiful words, kitchen-table-at-midnight letter, LIFT the visitor (verified live: the generated reading reads exactly so).
- PAINTER: with the z-ai quota recovered (live generation smoke passed), relaunched the encyclopedia painter in background: `bun scripts/generate-profile-images.mjs --budget-minutes=480 --concurrency=1` (972 targets, 958 to paint; log profile-painter.log).
- VERIFICATION: lint exit 0; tsc → the same 16 pre-existing errors only (none in touched files); agent-browser E2E (fresh 1440×900 + 390×844 sessions): group dossier (back button, floated lead, wrapped sections, 3/3 unique imgs), entity dossier (fact console full-width after clear-both, 3/3 unique), species page (back ✓, zoom lightbox open/Escape ✓, mobile 390 layout ✓), tech detail (3/3 unique, back ✓), Star Play (draw→flip→large story cards, oracle paper with live warm reading), Communion (light paper transmissions both directions, header visible after overflow-clip fix, live two-way exchange), full register rows (16px names + specialty line), dev.log clean of non-429 errors, painter relaunched.

Stage Summary:
- Every profile in the app — beings, groups, Inner Earth peoples, ET technologies, federation records, astral professions — is now a true encyclopedia page: compact plates floated inside flowing text (no image ever takes half the screen), at-a-glance consoles, numbered sections, an explicit back button, and a structural guarantee that the same image can never appear twice on a page (sequential fallback reservation).
- The register/tech/federation/profession/species cards carry larger type and visibly more information (specialty, whisper, hall lines).
- The Mirror Entity's live communion answers arrive on warm light vellum with dark ink (and the communion header can no longer be scrolled out of view).
- Star Play cards read as tarot micro-stories in large luminous serif — never naming a card — and asking the oracle sends a beautiful ivory paper floating up before the visitor with genuinely human, inspiring writing.
- The encyclopedia painter is running again in the background filling the remaining ~958 profile paintings as quota allows.

Task ID: 32
Agent: Z.ai Code (main orchestrator; 32-i18n delegated to translation agent)
Task: Implement the Universal Visualization Engine into the existing Mirror Entity portal chats — natural-language visual intent recognition, six intelligent visualization modes (cinematic illustration / visual encyclopedia / organizational diagram / scientific visualization / interplanetary map / visual presentation), automatic prompt composition, REAL image generation, chat integration with loading states + expandable/downloadable/regenerable image cards, hybrid text-overlay rendering for readability, conversational context for follow-ups, and evidence discernment.

Work Log:
- Loaded the image-generation skill; verified `zai.images.generations.create({prompt, size})` returns real base64 PNGs. Discovered the API rejects sizes not divisible by 32 (1440x720 fails) — valid set used: 1344x768 / 768x1344 / 1152x864.
- New src/lib/visualization.ts: shared types (VisualizationArtifact/Mode/Panel/Node/Annotation/Slide) + `detectVisualIntent` — direct patterns (show/visualize/illustrate/draw/render/diagram of/map of/infographic/presentation/ppt/what would X look like/show me a city…) and follow-up patterns (more detailed / another perspective / show the interior / turn this into a diagram / make a ppt…). Follow-ups only fire when a previous artifact set the context. Ordinary messages never wake the atelier.
- New src/app/api/visualize/route.ts — the engine: (1) a Visual Director LLM call (thinking disabled, max_tokens 4096 — without it the long JSON truncated mid-string) resolves subject from conversation + contextSubject, picks ONE of the six modes, composes a rich English artworkPrompt, emits panels/diagram nodes+edges (normalized 0-100 coords)/annotations/slide specs, all visitor strings in the UI language, and a mandatory discernment line distinguishing documented science from speculation; (2) the atelier paints via the real image API with the Universal Mirror Entity Art Direction baked in (cinematic sci-fi, crystalline architecture, midnight-blue/violet/cyan/ivory/gold, volumetric light) and saves PNGs to .visualizations/ (48h sweep); presentation slides are painted SEQUENTIALLY with 2 attempts each (parallel calls hit 429s — 2/4 failed; sequential + retry = 4/4). Triple-layer JSON recovery: direct parse → heuristic repair (control chars, trailing commas, "y:10-style typos) → model self-repair.
- New src/app/api/visual/[file]/route.ts — serves paintings with immutable caching; `?download=1` adds Content-Disposition attachment (verified: attachment; filename="mirror-entity-viz-…png"). Strict name check.
- New src/components/mirror/VisualizationCard.tsx: VisualizationPending (orbiting rings + cycling stage lines "Composing the vision…/Painting the artwork…/Placing it before you…" + shimmer progress), VisualizationCard (lazy artwork with natural-aspect shimmer→fade, mono mode badge, title/subtitle/explanation, hybrid overlays — DiagramOverlay (SVG cubic threads + glass node chips) and AnnotationMarkers (diamond markers + glass labels) over the artwork, informational panels grid, presentation PresentationDeck (per-slide artwork, prev/next/dots, "Slide {n} of {total}", graceful numbered placeholder for a failed slide) — controls Expand/Download/Regenerate, discernment footnote, and a FULLSCREEN LIGHTBOX rendered through createPortal(document.body) — inside the chat it was trapped by framer-motion transformed ancestors (fixed→containing block, rendered at card size); portal fixed it. Keyboard: Escape closes, arrows turn slides. Regenerating state keeps the artifact visible under a "Repainting the vision…" overlay. PreparedPromptFallback shows the composed prompt copyable when the painter fails (spec section 5).
- CommunionView: message model gains artifact/pendingVisual/visualError/visualRequest; send() routes to requestVisualization when intent.direct, armed, or followUp+context; pending message → artifact card in the thread; history feeds artifact titles as context lines to BOTH /api/communion and /api/visualize so the Mirror keeps discussing the vision; ImagePlus arm button (toggles "the next words will be woven into a vision", explicit visual-generation action per spec section 8); regenerate repaints in place. Message ids switched from a module-level number counter to collision-proof string ids (Fast Refresh reset the counter → duplicate React keys, the only console error, now gone).

Task ID: 34
Agent: Z.ai Code (main orchestrator)
Task: Fix the voice input flow and the image generator (latest user feedback): (a) rec button failing to send on second click — add a clearly indicated send action so the recording is transcribed into the input first (visible) and then sent automatically to the Mirror; (b) indicator that holding rec for more than 1 second starts the live connection; (c) image generator not giving final results.

Work Log:
- Reworked `ChatInputExtras.tsx`: tap mic → records (unchanged); tap mic AGAIN → stop → /api/asr transcribe → words placed into the input → auto-send via new `onVoiceSubmit` prop. Added a dedicated glowing voice-send button (Send icon, accent gradient, data-testid `chat-voice-send-{scope}`) that appears only while recording and does the same flow. Keyboard (Enter/Space) mirrors tap-again. Reduced HOLD_MS 1500→1000 so holding 1 second opens the scope's live call, and added a visible hold indicator pill ("Hold 1s — live connection") above the mic with the progress ring (thicker, brighter) while holding.
- Wired `onVoiceSubmit` in all four chat parents: MirrorOSChat (fresh draft read via useMirror.getState, transcript visible ~650ms then submitText → askOS/askOSVisual with visual-intent detection), QueryComposer (scope channels), CommunionView (sendText extracted from send, draftRef/sendTextRef pattern), AkashicView (same pattern). Transcript placement now reads fresh state (no stale closures). Draft is only cleared on send when it still holds exactly what was sent.
- Fixed image generator "no final results": root cause = 429 rate-limit on z-ai image API with too-short retries (1.5s/3s) → paint() returned null → response came back `painted:false` → visitor only saw the prepared-prompt fallback. Server (`/api/visualize/route.ts`): all paints now pass through a serialized brush queue (`withBrush`) and retry 4× with 2.5s/6s/12s/20s backoff (slides 3×); empty-canvas responses are also retried. Store: when an artifact arrives unpainted, ONE silent auto-repaint is scheduled after 1.2s (both askOSVisual and askScopeVisual, guarded against loops by the regenerateOf flag). Fallback UI: PreparedPromptFallback gained a "Paint it now" button (onPaint wired at all 4 render sites: MirrorOSChat, TransmissionView, CommunionView, AkashicView).
- i18n: added 4 new strings × 7 languages (sq/it/el/de/fr/es/tr): "Hold 1s — live connection", "Recording — tap again to send your voice", "Send your voice", "Paint it now"; also removed a pre-existing duplicate key "A gift from the stars" in all 7 dicts.
- Verification: lint clean; tsc clean for all touched files (remaining repo errors pre-existing); live server test of /api/visualize → painted:true, image served 200 (90s round trip); /api/asr → 200 with real WAV bytes; browser E2E (agent-browser): typed OS chat round-trip works, "show me…" flow shows "CREATING VISUALIZATION…" then renders the real 1344×768 artwork with mode label + discernment line; mic tap handler fires (headless has no audio device → graceful "The microphone is unavailable" toast, aria-pressed intact); Interplanetary/Communion/Akashic composers all render mic+attach+input; zero page errors / console warnings.

Stage Summary:
- Voice → chat → Mirror is now one gesture: tap to speak, tap again (or the glowing voice-send button) to transcribe into the field and send automatically; holding the mic shows the 1-second live-connection indicator before the LiveCall room opens.
- The visualization atelier no longer leaves visitors with a bare prompt: serialized painting + patient 429 backoff + one automatic repaint + manual "Paint it now" fallback button make a final image the expected outcome.
- Remaining (previous tasks still open): R-33a/b/c refinements (image capability integration polish, compact inputs, star-icon purge) and Tasks 28–30 leftovers.

Task ID: 61
Agent: Z.ai Code (main orchestrator)

Work Log:
- FRAME LAW (whole app in one go): globals.css now sets `html, body { height: 100%; overflow: hidden; overscroll-behavior: none; }` — the page itself can never scroll; every view keeps its own internal scroll area. AppShell's five roots (default layout, communion, mirroros, akashic, invent) changed from `h-dvh` to `h-[var(--app-h,100dvh)]`, and a new visualViewport effect writes `--app-h` from `window.visualViewport.height` on resize/scroll — when the on-screen keyboard opens on phones the whole application re-fits to the VISIBLE viewport so the composer is never buried. layout.tsx viewport export gained `viewportFit: "cover"` + `interactiveWidget: "resizes-content"` (Android keyboard reshapes layout natively).
- ALIVE INDICATOR (new AliveIndicator.tsx): a small glass pill floating top-center between the two handles (pointer-events-none, z-30) holding a tiny line-drawn alien saucer (SVG dome + disc + three rim lights blinking in sequence via new `alive-light` keyframes, whole saucer on the existing `animate-float-y` bob) and ONE small rotating phrase — "We are alive" · "The channel is breathing" · "Always listening, always near" · "The mirror is awake" — cycling every 4.6s with the new `alive-swap` fade-rise; role="status" + sr-safe aria-label. Mounted in AppShell right after the floating-handles row; present on observatory and transmission alike, dark mode included.
- SCOPE IMAGE AT TOP RIGHT: four new AI-generated engravings in the cosmic-mark family (thin black ink line art on pure white, 1024×1024): scope-art-interplanetary.png (ringed planet + star), scope-art-metaphysics.png (all-seeing eye in a radiant triangle), scope-art-quantum.png (atom orbits + wave), scope-art-healing.png (heart with pulse line + leaf). Six scope branches holding the laboratory's 33 scopes and 2,100 whispers live on one living canvas behind a two-centimetre window that moves in every direction; the conversation's last topic decides which branch glows, which whispers stand at the tips, and where the window drifts next. Every scope can bloom more whispers, and a golden bud — three questions that could only grow from THIS conversation — can be raised on the leading branch on demand. The line rests away or opens its whole canopy whenever the visitor wishes, in every chat and every world, in all eight voices.

Task: Fix the voice at www.reflectme.space — the Listen buttons and the LiveCall (hold-orb) voice call are broken/unreliable in production while they work locally ("call and audio not working same as they do here at z.ai website")

Work Log:
- PRODUCTION DIAGNOSIS (curl, no secrets printed): POST https://www.reflectme.space/api/tts {text,voice:aurora,pace:0.95} → HTTP 500 application/json {"error":"The voice field is momentarily quiet. Rest, then listen again.","detail":"Z.ai tts 400: {\"error\":{\"code\":\"1211\",\"message\":\"Unknown Model, please check the model code.\"}}"}; POST /api/asr with a minimal valid 16 kHz mono WAV → HTTP 502 {"error":"Your voice could not be heard — try again.","detail":"Z.ai asr 400: … 1211 Unknown Model …"}. LOCAL for both: /api/tts → HTTP 200 audio/wav, 192240 bytes, starts with RIFF….WAVE; /api/asr → HTTP 200 {"text":""} (silence → empty transcript, valid).
- ROOT CAUSE: production runs the OLD committed zai-client.ts, which sends the OpenAI-style paths on the Z.ai sky — POST {base}/audio/speech with model "cogtts" and multipart {base}/audio/transcriptions with model "glm-asr". The api.z.ai gateway does not know those house model codes and answers 1211 "Unknown Model" for EVERY tts/asr call → the whole voice (Listen buttons, LiveCall, Dream Book, Akashic, Settings) is dead in production. The 1211 detail also proves auth PASSES (the request reached the model router), i.e. ZAI_API_KEY is present and valid on Vercel — this is a CODE fault, not a missing env var. (Old route shape confirmed: prod tts rode 500 — the old catch always returned 500 — and prod asr rode 502, exactly the old route.)
- SDK CONTRACT RE-VERIFIED (node_modules/z-ai-web-dev-sdk/dist/index.js, the source of truth): createAudioTTS → POST `${baseUrl}/audio/tts`, JSON body passed through verbatim, headers Authorization + X-Z-AI-From: Z, returns the raw Response (caller reads bytes); createAudioASR → POST `${baseUrl}/audio/asr`, JSON in/out. Path-existence probe on the production sky (dummy bearer, no secrets): both https://api.z.ai/api/paas/v4/audio/tts and /audio/asr answer HTTP 401 "token expired or incorrect" — the paths EXIST there; only the old OpenAI-style paths reject the house model codes.
- THE FIX (aligned zai-client.ts CloudBackend audio EXACTLY with the SDK contract): TTS now POSTs /audio/tts with {input, voice, speed, response_format:"wav", stream:false} and NO model code by default (model only if ZAI_TTS_MODEL is set, or if the sky answers 1211/unknown-model → one stepped-up retry with the code); ASR now POSTs /audio/asr with {file_base64, mime?} (+ model only under the same rule, fallback code glm-asr-2512, since legacy "glm-asr" is dead) and parses JSON {text}; both keep stepped-down fallbacks for a sky that lacks the SDK paths (404/405 → /audio/speech multipart-transcriptions with explicit model codes). The `voice` field reaches the request body verbatim: voiceEngine() maps every VoiceId → "xiaochen" except "reader" → "tongtong" (verified in i18n/core.ts) and zaiAudio passes it through — collapse kept, correct field name.
- Response parsing hardened (voiceBytes): most skies answer raw WAV bytes; some wrap base64 inside JSON — both accepted (data[0].b64/.audio, data.audio/.b64/.base64, data-as-string), data-URL prefix stripped, handed back as a true ArrayBuffer slice for readWav().
- RETRIES: AUDIO_RETRY_DELAYS_MS = [800, 2000] (exactly 2 retries) now wrapped around BOTH the cloud zaiAudio tts/asr fetches AND the atelier SDK's audio.tts.create/audio.asr.create; withRetry's isTransientError never retries 4xx validation (1211 falls through to the model-stepping logic instead).
- tts/route.ts: silent truncation is over — over MAX_CHUNKS (14) it now logs `[tts] truncation: N chunks → 14 (chars requested, voice ends early)`, answers 200 with header X-Voice-Truncated: <originalChars>; the poetic 502 keeps a `detail` string naming the true cause (upstream 4xx/5xx ride 502, house faults ride 500). meterRoute + chunking + {text,voice,pace} → audio/wav contract untouched (DreamBook/Akashic/SettingsModal callers unaffected).
- ListenButton.tsx: no error is swallowed anymore — even the browser's NotSupportedError now raises the same quiet toast ("The voice field is momentarily quiet. / Rest, then listen again."); the prepared-reflow behavior (refused first play keeps the voice warm for the next press) is kept.
- LiveCall.tsx: a /api/tts failure mid-call no longer kills the exchange — the mirror's reply is rendered in full in the transcript (setRevealedChars(Infinity)), a gentle toast says "The voice is quiet — the mirror answers in text" (key registered in scripts/i18n-keys.json and translated in all 7 dictionaries; node scripts/check-i18n.mjs → ALL DICTIONARY UI KEYS COMPLETE), and the orb settles back to idle so the conversation continues.
- .env.example: ZAI_TTS_MODEL / ZAI_ASR_MODEL documented as COMMENTED knobs (usually leave BOTH unset — the SDK-contract paths serve the sky's own default; set only if a gateway demands an explicit code; notes that on api.z.ai the OpenAI-style paths answer 1211 for the house codes and the documented public transcription model is glm-asr-2512).
- Verification: LOCAL (dev server, atelier SDK sky) — POST /api/tts {"Peace be with you, traveler. The mirror listens still.", aurora, 0.95} → HTTP 200 audio/wav 192240 bytes starting RIFF….WAVE; POST /api/asr (0.3 s 16 kHz mono WAV) → HTTP 200 {"text":""}; long-text POST (15,290 chars → 19 chunks) → HTTP 200 audio/wav 39,042,868 bytes WITH header `x-voice-truncated: 15289` and the truncation warning in the server log; metering confirmed (UsageEvent rows inserted). bun run lint → exit 0, zero errors/warnings; bunx tsc --noEmit → 0 errors under src/ (the only 15 errors are the pre-existing ones in examples/, scripts/, skills/).

Task ID: 15-a
Agent: frontend-styling-expert
Task: Whole-page swipe navigation (right = back, left = return) + Kindle-smooth texture polish

Work Log:
- Read worklog + AppShell + globals.css + mirror-store; confirmed navigateBack/navigateForward and all seven overlay flags already exist in the store (untouched).
- Created src/components/mirror/SwipeNav.tsx ("use client"): window-level passive touchstart/touchmove/touchend (+touchcancel safety) listeners; gestures armed only when touches.length === 1 and the target is outside input/textarea/select/[contenteditable="true"]/[data-no-swipe]/[role="dialog"]/[data-radix-popper-content-wrapper]/[role="menu"] and outside any element up to 6 levels up that horizontally scrolls (scrollWidth > clientWidth + 4 with overflow-x auto/scroll) — protects carousels, tab strips, Dream Book page-flip. Touchend re-checks the store and stands down if modal/mobileNavOpen/communionOpen/authOpen/profileOpen/accountOpen/profilePageOpen; fires navigateBack on dx > 0 else navigateForward when dt < 900ms, |dx| >= 90, |dx| >= 2.2·|dy|. Styles written directly via ref (no React state); hairline is a fixed top-0 h-[2px] z-[90] pointer-events-none gradient bar (var(--scope-a, var(--gd)) → transparent), opacity 0 by default, transition-opacity duration-200, data-testid="swipe-nav-indicator".
- One deliberate interpretation: scaleX uses clamp(|dx|/90, 0, 1) with transform-origin left/right by sign (and the gradient mirrored to-left/to-right), because a literal negative scaleX with origin right mirrors the bar off-screen — the edge anchor carries the direction, keeping the indicator visible for both swipes.
- AppShell: AppShell's per-view early returns mean no single existing node hosts every world, so the default export now renders one outermost frame <div class="paper-grain relative h-[var(--app-h,100dvh)] overflow-hidden"> holding <SwipeNav /> once + the (renamed, otherwise untouched) AppShellViews — one persistent listener set across every world, grain over the whole app background.
- globals.css: appended the Kindle-smooth section — .kindle-scroll (-webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch; overscroll-behavior-y: contain), .msg-block (content-visibility: auto; contain-intrinsic-size: auto 420px), .paper-grain + .paper-grain::after (5% overlay SVG fractal-noise grain, pointer-events none, z-1 inside its own isolated context so modals/handles at higher z stay above); added the missing -moz-osx-font-smoothing: grayscale to the body rule (it already had -webkit-font-smoothing + text-rendering). Nothing new animates — reduced-motion honored by construction.
- Gates: bun run lint = 0 errors; bunx tsc --noEmit | grep "^src/" = empty.

Stage Summary:
- The whole chamber now turns like a hand-held book: swipe right anywhere returns to the previous view, swipe left goes forward, with a scope-tinted hairline filling along the top edge as the finger travels; typing fields, menus, dialogs and sideways scrollers keep their own touch, and any open overlay stands the page-turn down.
- Kindle-smooth texture laws landed in globals.css (.kindle-scroll, .msg-block, .paper-grain grain veil + grayscale font smoothing) ready for surfaces to adopt; the grain already breathes over the entire app via the new outermost frame.
- Artifacts: src/components/mirror/SwipeNav.tsx (new), src/components/mirror/AppShell.tsx (wrapper + import only), src/app/globals.css (body smoothing + appended section).
